【甲骨文敦促監管機構加快審批資料中心天然氣管道專案】甲骨文再次請求聯邦監管機構儘快批准一條天然氣管道，以滿足其在新墨西哥州大型資料中心專案的供電需求。甲骨文政府事務高階副總裁Josh Pitcock週二在致美國聯邦能源監管委員會的信中指出，如果該委員會繼續推遲批准由Energy Transfer LP旗下子公司運營的管道專案，將“威脅到木星專案（Project Jupiter）的運營進度，進而影響由此帶來的就業機會、經濟活動及社群效益”。這條名為Green Chile的管道將每天輸送約4億立方英尺天然氣，為Bloom Energy公司提供的燃料電池供能，以支援木星專案執行。目前，該管道的投運時間已推遲至2027年2月，遠晚於此前計劃的8月15日投運日期。

【Houthi missile strike hits ARAMCO Dhahran HQ; partial fire reported】Six people familiar with the matter said on Tuesday that Houthi forces launched a missile strike that hit ARAMCO’s headquarters in Dhahran in eastern Saudi Arabia, causing a partial fire. One source said the blaze was later extinguished. The extent of damage is unclear and it is not yet known whether the fire was caused by intercepted debris. The Saudi-led coalition conducting operations in Yemen said Houthi missiles struck multiple Saudi cities including Riyadh and Dhahran, and that debris from intercepts fell into a residential area in Dhahran. Dhahran, on Saudi Arabia’s Persian Gulf coast, is at the center of the country’s oil industry.

### 2026-10-14 05:02:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Inflation Rate MoM #Importance 1

Argentina Inflation Rises in September — Argentina’s consumer price index rose 1.9% month-over-month in September 2026, up from 1.7% in August, and accumulated 23.6% year-to-date. Housing, water, electricity, gas and other fuels recorded the largest monthly increase at 3.1%, followed by education at 3.0%. Restaurants and hotels posted the smallest increase at 1.1%, while clothing and footwear rose 1.4%. Food and non-alcoholic beverages made the largest contribution to monthly inflation across regions, except in Greater Buenos Aires, where housing and utilities had the greatest impact. Core CPI rose 1.7%, driven in part by higher prices for dining out and pharmaceutical products. Seasonal prices increased 2.9%, led by vegetables, tubers and legumes, while regulated prices rose 2.3%, reflecting higher gas, electricity and private healthcare costs.

### 2026-10-14 04:57:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Drops as Inflation Forecast Rises — The Ibovespa fell 1% to close at 206,887 on Tuesday, weighed down by losses in major banks, utilities and mining stocks. The Focus survey raised its 2026 inflation forecast to 5.22% from 5.01%, marking a fourth consecutive increase, while the Selic benchmark rate remained unchanged at 13.25%. The IMF also cut its 2026 GDP growth forecast for Brazil to 2.2% from 2.4%, citing a slowdown expected in 2026–2027 due to restrictive interest rates aimed at bringing inflation back to target, the withdrawal of fiscal stimulus and global uncertainty. Major banks declined, with Itaú falling 1.6% and Bradesco losing 1%. Utilities also retreated, with Axia down 1.6% and Sabesp shedding 3.1%. Mining stocks weakened as ample global supply, high Chinese inventories and lower shipping costs continued to weigh on iron ore prices. Vale fell 1.1%. In contrast, Petrobras gained 1.5% as oil prices rebounded.

【Saudi national arrested after concealed recorder found in OpenAI meeting room】OpenAI and the U.S. Department of Justice said a Saudi national, Abdulrahman Alfozan, was arrested on Oct. 9 on criminal charges after a concealed recording device was found behind a television in an executive meeting room. Alfozan, who joined OpenAI’s product team in July 2025, has been detained by federal authorities and was fired. OpenAI said it used its AI models to analyze the device and identify the person who placed it; the room is regularly used for confidential discussions of research, product plans, commercial strategy, government affairs and business relationships. The company reported the matter to the FBI, which carried out the arrest, and said there is no evidence the individual improperly accessed or stole model weights, source code or customer data. The investigation is ongoing.

TSX Rises as Banks and Energy Stocks Advance — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose 0.9% to close at 35,984 on Tuesday as Canadian bond yields stabilized. The pause in the recent rally that had pushed yields to multi-year highs supported credit-sensitive sectors such as banking. RBC rose 1.3%, TD Bank gained 2.2%, BMO advanced 1.3%, and Scotiabank and CIBC added 1.8% each. Energy producers also advanced as oil prices erased earlier losses. Canadian Natural jumped 4.8%, Suncor rose 3.7%, Imperial Oil gained 4.5% and Cenovus advanced 4.2%. In technology, Shopify and Constellation Software rose 3% each amid gains in US hyperscalers, while Celestica fell 1.7%, tracking losses among chipmakers. Elsewhere, CSG acquired a 9.5% stake in MDA Space, becoming its largest shareholder; MDA gained 1.1%. Miners declined as gold prices fell, with Barrick down 1.4% and Agnico Eagle and WPM shedding 2.1% each.

### 2026-10-14 04:30:26  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #US Economy #DXY #Impact bullish #S&P500 #Impact bearish #WTI

【COLLINS: Further rate hikes this year may be needed; watch for inflation broadening】Boston Fed President COLLINS said policy rates may need to rise further before year-end to curb inflation, and any additional hikes and timing will depend on incoming economic data. She expects US growth to continue and the labor market to remain near full employment; under that baseline, productivity gains should help rebalance demand and supply and bring inflation toward 2%. She warned two other scenarios are nearly as likely: AI-driven investment could spur stronger-than-expected spending, overheating the labor market and keeping inflation above target; and tariffs or higher energy prices—especially if conflict involving Iran continues—could sustain price pressure. COLLINS said it is important to watch whether high inflation remains concentrated in energy and AI-infrastructure sectors or begins to broaden.

Brent Crude Prices Rise as Supply Risks Mount — Brent crude pared earlier losses to trade over $106.5 a barrel on Tuesday amid concerns over supply risks linked to the conflict between Saudi Arabia and the Iran-backed Houthis. The militants have stepped up attacks on Saudi Arabia since last week, targeting energy facilities and airports. The Houthis attacked King Khalid International Airport in Riyadh on Saturday, while a Saudi-led coalition said it intercepted a ballistic missile fired toward the capital on Monday. Meanwhile, Iran is fortifying sensitive sites, including nuclear facilities, and rallying supporters amid concerns over possible renewed US attacks after the November midterm elections. In contrast, prospects for additional Russian energy supplies to global markets limited oil’s gains. An agreement would allow Russia to supply diesel to the US and other markets for six months. However, Goldman Sachs said the additional Russian diesel supplies were unlikely to significantly ease pressure on global fuel prices.

【Bank of America fined $1.2bn by US Treasury for AML, sanctions failures】The US Treasury fined Bank of America (BAC.N) $1.2bn for “serious and persistent” anti‑money‑laundering and sanctions compliance failures, saying the bank failed to report suspicious transactions totaling more than $5bn. The penalty includes a record $750m civil fine from the OCC; the Office of Foreign Assets Control reached an $11.6m settlement over alleged violations tied to Iran, Russia, drug trafficking and global terrorism sanctions. Regulators required Bank of America to enter a consent order that mandates third‑party remediation to identify and report previously undetected suspicious transactions. The action is a regulatory hit ahead of the bank’s upcoming Q3 results.

### 2026-10-14 04:14:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Uruguay #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Uruguay Industrial Production Rises in August — Uruguay’s industrial production rose 4.8% year over year in August 2026. The increase was driven primarily by a 136.1% surge in petroleum refining, contributing 3.8 percentage points to overall growth, partly reflecting a low base as ANCAP’s refinery was offline for repairs during part of August 2025. Food products manufacturing rose 5.4%, contributing 2.3 percentage points, while paper and paper products increased 4.4%. In contrast, beverage manufacturing slumped 30.5%, making the largest negative contribution, followed by computer, electronic and optical products (-90.1%) and chemicals (-5.6%). Meanwhile, the Index of Hours Worked rose 0.3%, while the Employment Index fell 0.9%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production increased 0.4%. Year to date, output rose 3.3%.

### 2026-10-14 03:31:31  #市場 #國際

【Rosenberg稱美股越來越像泡沫 多項指標極端程度從業40年來未見】David Rosenberg表示，美國股市越來越像一個泡沫，估值、槓桿和投資者倉位均處於其從業40年來前所未見的極端水平。Rosenberg是總部位於多倫多的Rosenberg Research & Associates創始人。他認為，當前牛市對人工智慧(AI)進展走向的依賴程度，遠高於網際網路泡沫形成前夕股市對科技行業的依賴程度。他表示，自己一直在投資組合中買入美國國債和海外股票，並稱美國是投資者正在為之支付過高價格的“科技繁榮中心”。“我從業40年，不記得什麼時候見過像今天這樣如此多的分化、極端現象和失衡，不僅是在市場上，也包括經濟領域，”他在紐約舉行的Grant’s Annual Fall Conference上表示，“如果你不想讓我稱之為泡沫，我可以不這麼叫。但我現在看到的這些情況，確實是以前從未見過的。”

### 2026-10-14 03:20:17  #市場

【美債期貨負凸性升至近20年高位 MOVE指數創立者警告“噩夢情景”】美債市場MOVE指數的創立者Harley Bassman警告稱，債市一個鮮受關注的特徵可能令拋售行情更為劇烈，並進一步推高債券收益率。Bassman在其Convexity Maven通訊中發文稱，這一風險源於期貨合約結算時，交易員提供交割債券的方式。他說，近期收益率大幅躍升對較長期債券的打擊最大，期限最長的債券根據所謂的“最便宜可交割”(cheapest-to-deliver)規則成為用於履行期貨合約交割義務的債券。但這些債券的久期也更高，這意味著利率上升時，它們面臨更大的下跌風險。為了對沖這種風險，基金經理可能會在合約到期前賣出其他長期債券，從而形成一種在價格已經下跌時進一步放大賣壓的機制。Bassman表示，與債券期貨相關的負凸性，也就是其異常突出的下行風險，已經升至2007年以來最高水平，債市最近一輪拋售背後可能有其推波助瀾。

### 2026-10-14 03:16:39  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #US10Y #Impact bearish #DXY #Impact bullish #S&P500

【Dalio: US bond bear market far from over; supply-demand imbalance to push yields higher】Bridgewater founder Ray Dalio said the US bond sell-off is far from over as rising government and corporate funding needs and weaker demand from some traditional buyers intensify pressure on Treasuries. He warned the US is entering a phase in which higher bond supply will push yields up, and rising borrowing costs will further enlarge debt — a cycle that would only end if high rates weaken the economy and financial markets enough to rebalance supply and demand. Dalio said US debt is at its highest level since WWII and is likely to worsen in coming years. He noted the government expects to roll about $10 tln of maturing debt this year and to add roughly $2.1 tln in new borrowing. He cautioned demand from major foreign Treasury holders could weaken while corporate bond and equity issuance to fund AI infrastructure competes with Treasuries for capital. He also said signs in corporate credit show investors are demanding higher returns to absorb new supply. Given rising supply and weakening demand, Dalio said shorting Treasuries remains more attractive than going long.

Heating Oil Surges Nearly 5% — Heating oil futures in the US surged to $4.92 per gallon on Tuesday, the highest in three weeks and close to last month's record high of $5.30, as unprecedented supply tightness returned to the market's forefront. Russia and Ukraine exchanged strikes against each other's power and gas facilities. The strikes erased already-dim expectations that the energy ceasefire touted by President Trump would hold, stoking fears of continued attacks on major Russian refineries. The developments also deepened doubts that Russia would release 36 million barrels of diesel for export, a pledge that faced skepticism upon announcement, as a domestic distillate shortage prompted the Moscow to halt exports last quarter. Meanwhile, attacks on vessels crossing the Persian Gulf kept tanker shipping costs near record highs and curtailed exports from the region. Shortages of crude oil for major refineries had already driven prices to a record high and prompted US officials to briefly consider an export ban.

### 2026-10-14 00:54:50  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Gasoline Holds Decline — US gasoline futures held near $3.25 a gallon, extending their downward trend since October 9th as traders continue to weigh the impact of fuel supplies from Russia. While Russia had agreed to supply diesel to global markets to help lower fuel prices, experts believe the deal announced by President Trump to be very small, equal to just about 10 days of total US demand or slightly more than a single day of global demand. Prospects of a Russia–Ukraine energy ceasefire also remain in focus as Russia continued to attack Ukrainian energy facilities, contrary to President Trump's claims of an immediate truce. Both Ukraine and Germany have pushed for strike bans to include Ukrainian electricity infrastructure as well as Russian refineries. Attacks on tankers crossing the Strait of Hormuz also remain risks to feedstock supply. Against this backdrop, pump prices remain elevated, with 12.1% of global refining capacity offline as of September, although prices have eased in recent weeks.

European Stocks Close Lower — European stocks erased early gains and closed firmly lower on Tuesday amid sharp losses for the AI infrastructure sector and heavyweight industrials. The Euro STOXX 50 fell 0.6% to 6,170 and the STOXX Europe 600 eased 0.2% to 635. ASML slid 4.2% ahead of its earnings report this week, tracking sharp losses for chip producers across major Western economies after Chinese chipmaker CXMT said it achieved a breakthrough in its memory architecture that rivals Korean and US competitors. Meanwhile, LVMH closed slightly lower after its fashion and leather goods revenue fell 5% year-over-year in the third quarter due to slower sales in China. The developments prompted analysts and brokers to downgrade recommendations on Hermès, pushing the stock 2% lower. On the other hand, banks were mostly higher, with UniCredit and Nordea adding nearly 1%.

Hermès dropped 1.9% after UBS cut its price target, while Kering shed 1.4% after TD Cowen lowered its target. ### 2026-10-13 23:59:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures at 1-Month High — Cocoa prices rose further to around $5,800 a tonne, their highest level since mid-September, supported by weather-related supply concerns. Local reports indicated that heavy rainfall in the Ivory Coast, the world’s largest cocoa producer, had disrupted harvesting and delayed bean deliveries to ports, limiting near-term availability. However, both the Ivory Coast and Ghana have received below-average rainfall in recent months, raising the risk of stress on the next crop. Local farmers remained worried about the size and quality of the next cocoa crop, which is expected to be harvested from early 2027. The possible strengthening of El Niño adds to these concerns, as it could bring hotter, drier weather to West Africa and increase the risk of heavy rain and flooding in parts of Latin America, including Ecuador. Meanwhile, third-quarter cocoa-grinding data from Europe and North America, due on October 15, will provide further insight into demand.

Financial stocks mostly outperformed, supported by a retreat in global bond yields and upbeat sentiment following record third-quarter earnings from major US lenders. MPS gained 0.5% after its shareholder meeting provided no further updates on its counterbid for Intesa Sanpaolo (0.2%). Unipol, Banca Mediolanum and BPER Banca each advanced over 1%, while Banco BPM fell 1.5% and Azimut shed 1.7%.Tech stocks fell as concerns of overspending ahead of earnings reports extended a selloff in chipmakers, while a breakthrough from China's CXMT added to the sector’s headwinds. Technoprobe led the index’s losses, falling 5.1%, followed by STMicroelectronics (-3.9%) and Prysmian (-2.2%). Stellantis fell 1.1% despite the autofirm's growing confidence in their EVs at the 2026 Paris auto show. Luxury Moncler fell 0.6% following LVMH's quarterly sales miss, underscoring weak demand from China.

US Stocks Erase Gains — US stock indices pulled back from session highs on Tuesday as Treasuries reversed gains and pressured AI infrastructure names alongside major earnings. Chipmakers extended declines on reports that China's CXMT advanced new memory architecture, threatening US and Korean rivals. Broadcom CEO Hock Tan also warned of excessive AI lab spending yielding poor returns, sending Micron, Marvell, and SanDisk down 4% to 6%. In turn, J&J sank 5.5% on soft sales, while UNH gained 2% on an upgraded profit outlook. Financials saw uneven results, with Citigroup falling 2% despite posting strong revenues and Wells Fargo slipping 1.4% after publishing results. ### 2026-10-13 23:27:48  #MKT News #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Strait of Hormuz shipping risk rises as more than a dozen VLCCs turn back near Fujairah】Iranian attacks on vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz have surged, potentially threatening the recent rebound in Persian Gulf oil exports. Shipping sources say cargo volumes via the strait earlier this month recovered close to pre-conflict levels, but recent signs point to a possible slowdown. There is no clear evidence that crude loading at several major producers has changed, and one owner said ship transits appear unrestricted. Aggregated tanker-tracking data show more than a dozen VLCCs and several Suezmaxes turned back or altered course near Fujairah, a major hub at the entrance to the strait. Brokers and some owners report additional slowdowns, notably longer wait times for ship-to-ship transfers, though an STS operator said his business had not been affected.

4、蘇德巍在電話會上表示，高盛會考慮在資產管理領域進行另一次收購——該領域近期已取得不少進展——但前提是財務條件合適，且更重要的是，必須與高盛文化契合。

Azerbaijan CPI Rises the Most in 8 Months — The CPI in Azerbaijan increased 0.9% month-over-month in September 2026, the most in eight months, following a 0.2% rise in August. Prices for food, alcoholic beverages and tobacco products went up 1.4%, with cost for buckwheat, beef, mutton and chicken, fresh and frozen fish, eggs, plums, peaches, chestnuts, greens, cucumbers, tomatoes, sweet peppers, eggplant, green beans, carrots, onions and potatoes rising. Prices for non-food products increased 0.4%, led by clothing, paint, sawn wood, furniture, laptops and tablet computers while prices for jewelry decreased. Year-on-year, the CPI rose 5.5%, slightly less than 5.7% in August.

US 10-Year Yield Erases Decline — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note erased its early pullback on Tuesday to hover above the 5.2% threshold, as markets continued to assess the magnitude of inflation risks from higher energy prices and the outlook for bond issuance by the tech sector. Energy benchmarks recovered in the session as the tight supply backdrop is likely to persist. Signals that Saudi Arabia would strike Houthi targets prolonged the conflict that jeopardizes tanker flows through the Red Sea, while evidence of low oil loadings at Russian Black Sea ports erased any signals that Russia would step up energy exports. Still, improvements in Chinese AI chip technology raised concerns of foreign competition for domestic semiconductor giants, limiting the outlook for corporate credit. The latest research showed industry players penning $4.1 trillion in AI-related debt to be issued by 2030, crowding out the Treasury in its issuance of new securities.

【OpenAI稱可攻克數學難題引學界爭議】近日，美國開放人工智慧研究中心OpenAI釋出700多篇由其內部模型生成的數學手稿，聲稱其模型能夠解決上百個數學難題，此舉在學界引發爭議。OpenAI聲稱，這些手稿解決了300多道人類之前無法攻克的數學難題，覆蓋代數、邏輯、幾何、機率等領域。這一事件引發廣泛關注，促使數學家思考人工智慧將如何衝擊、重塑數學研究。法國數學家塞德里克·維拉尼直言，這將是數學領域的一場變革。不過，有不少數學家對OpenAI宣稱的成果持懷疑態度。菲爾茲獎得主雨果·迪米尼-科潘表示，這批手稿涉及海量理論與資料，驗證工作量巨大。而OpenAI在釋出700多篇手稿後，也因為出現錯誤而撤回了3篇。還有學者指出，不少AI生成的證明方式不符合人類的思維習慣。（央視新聞）

【Earnings growth expectations cool; analysts warn limited tolerance for misses in overvalued US stocks】BofA aggregate data show S&P 500 Q3 earnings expectations remain strong, but 10 of 11 sectors are projected to see slower earnings growth versus the prior quarter. Slowing earnings growth alone may not halt the rally, but with US equities near record highs and growing concern that inflation could be stickier than expected, companies will need materially strong results to lift the market further. ### 2026-10-13 22:38:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Rise to Nearly 3-Week High — US natural gas prices climbed above $3.20 per million British thermal units on Tuesday, reaching their highest level since September 24, when prices touched $3.37, driven by lower production. Average output across the Lower 48 states fell to 110.6 billion cubic feet per day so far in October, down from record levels of 113.3 bcfd in August and September. The decline reflects production shutdowns caused by Hurricane Isaias, along with pipeline disruptions and force majeure incidents across several states. Daily output was expected to drop to around 107.1 bcfd on Tuesday, an eight-month low, although offshore production in the Gulf of Mexico is gradually recovering. By Monday, only 25% of offshore gas output remained shut in, compared with a peak of 59% on October 9. Meanwhile, LNG feedgas deliveries averaged 17.4 bcfd in October, below September’s 17.9 bcfd. Forecasts for milder weather and weaker demand over the next two weeks could limit further price gains.

【Escondida labour dispute escalates; over 1,000 workers may strike from Wednesday】Wage mediation between unions and operator BHP at Chile’s Escondida, the world’s largest copper mine, entered its final day after BHP revised its pay offer. Workers at an Antofagasta-owned copper unit have also returned to talks. Copper’s proximity to record highs has driven demands for greater profit sharing while miners push to contain cost inflation, stoking labour tensions and raising the risk of further supply disruption. BHP said its latest proposal would keep Escondida supervisors and staff among the highest paid in the sector; unions said it answered some demands but significant gaps remain. Union leader Alexis Barrera said a proposed signing bonus of about $25,500 per worker, presented as part of an overall benefits package, is below market given the company’s large profits. If no agreement is reached on Tuesday, more than 1,000 union members could start striking from Wednesday; mediation could also be extended.

【Russian crude exports under pressure as Black Sea loadings slow】Russian seaborne crude exports averaged 3.70 mln bpd in the four weeks to Oct 11, down 110k bpd from the four weeks to Oct 4, shipping-tracking data show. Operations at Black Sea ports have slowed amid heightened security concerns: satellite imagery of Novorossiysk’s Sheskharis terminal shows tankers occupying major loading berths for up to four days—about twice the loading time seen at comparable Baltic ports—reducing shipments. A fully laden tanker was struck by a drone after leaving Novorossiysk earlier last week, heightening owners’ and crews’ safety concerns. Russian authorities have rerouted all Kazakh crude exports through Sheskharis to free Ust-Luga berths for Russian cargoes, but higher Baltic shipments have not fully offset the Black Sea shortfall.

【High rates fail to curb lending; U.S. big-three banks post record net interest income】Despite higher rates, consumer and corporate borrowing kept pace, lifting JPMorgan, Citigroup and Wells Fargo to a combined Q3 net interest income of $56.5bn, the fifth consecutive quarter of record NII, results published Tuesday showed. JPMorgan said it expects net interest income to increase further in 2027, a contrast with earlier industry assumptions of falling rates when record trading revenue had drawn investor focus. All three banks reported declines in nonaccrual loans and said borrowers broadly remain able to service debt. BofA will report Q3 results on Wednesday. ### 2026-10-13 22:16:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Falls on Inflation and GDP Outlook — The Ibovespa fell over 0.5% to hover below 208,000 on Tuesday, weighed down by losses in major banks, utilities and mining stocks. The Focus survey raised its 2026 inflation forecast to 5.22% from 5.01%, marking a fourth consecutive increase, while the Selic benchmark rate remained unchanged at 13.25%. The IMF also lowered its growth projections for Brazil, cutting its 2026 GDP growth estimate to 2.2% from 2.4%. The fund attributed the slowdown expected between 2026 and 2027 to restrictive interest rates aimed at bringing inflation back to target, the withdrawal of fiscal stimulus and global uncertainty. Major banks declined, with Itaú down nearly 1.5% and Bradesco losing about 1%. Utilities also fell, with Sabesp down nearly 2%. Miners weakened as abundant global supply, elevated Chinese inventories and lower shipping costs continued to pressure iron ore prices. Vale shed nearly 1%.

【Microsoft rallies 50% since June, market value up $1.5tn; near record close】Microsoft (MSFT.O) has rallied more than 50% from its June low, adding roughly $1.5 trillion in market value and is poised to close at a record high for the first time in nearly a year. The fourth consecutive monthly advance has accelerated as investor confidence in Microsoft’s AI competitiveness improved. The stock was among the S&P 500’s worst performers in June, plunging 17% in its worst month since 2000 and erasing about $600 billion of market value. The reversal began after the July 29 earnings release, when cloud revenue growth hit its fastest pace in four years; the shares jumped 16% the next day and Microsoft posted its strongest quarterly performance since 1998. At least two firms have since upgraded the stock to buy, citing confidence in Microsoft’s ability to capture rising AI services demand.

US Existing Home Sales Remain Weak — Existing home sales in the United States rose by 1.5% from the two-year low in the previous month to a seasonally adjusted annualized rate of 3.99 million units in September of 2026, not too far from market expectations of 3.95 million. Total housing inventory reached 1.6 million units with the increase, 1.9% higher than the previous month to represent 4.8 months of supply under the latest sales rate. Sales recovered slightly in the South (2.8% to 1.86 million) and the West (2.8% to 0.73 million), offsetting a drop in the Northeast (-2.1% to 0.47 million) and a stall in the Midwest (at 0.93 million). “Despite the small monthly gain on a seasonally adjusted basis, August and September marked the two slowest months for home sales this year,” said NAR Chief Economist Lawrence Yun. “Further increases in mortgage rates in recent weeks mean fewer buyers can qualify to purchase a home.”

【未來五年 北京將建設100處古樹生境花園】古樹名木是鐫刻在京華大地上的綠色活化石，承載著古都北京千年生態記憶與歷史文脈。近日，首都綠化委員會印發《首都古樹名木保護高質量發展行動方案（2026—2030年）》，為“十五五”時期首都古樹名木保護管理事業高質量發展劃定清晰路線圖。根據方案，北京正式啟動首都古樹名木保護管理體系最佳化提升行動、古樹名木保護精準提質升級行動、古樹名木保護科技創新賦能行動和古樹名木文化傳承與活化利用四大行動，明確未來五年全市古樹名木保護的目標任務與實施路徑。力爭到2030年，北京將搭建1個標準化古樹名木資源資料庫，打造10處老城古樹生態文化地標，建設100處古樹生境花園、古樹村莊，對3000餘株古樹名木實施預防保護和搶救復壯，收集儲存100株珍稀瀕危古樹名木種質資源，推動古樹保護邁向更加科學化、數字化、體系化新階段。 (央視新聞)

【Handan says first Beijing low‑altitude economy firm relocates to Handan】Handan Investment Promotion Bureau said that after implementation of the capital no‑fly zone policy it matched an outward‑moving low‑altitude economy firm from the Beijing‑Tianjin‑Hebei region with Linzhang County and secured the relocation of Beijing Tianxun Chuangyi Technology Co. to Handan. The project comprises five modules — North China regional headquarters, final assembly/manufacturing, flight testing, operations & maintenance training, and scenario operations — to be rolled out in three phases. The initial phase will occupy a 4,000 sqm factory and targets annual production of 300 vertical take‑off and landing fixed‑wing UAVs. The first batch of equipment arrived on Oct. 12, the bureau said, marking Handan’s first Beijing low‑altitude economy relocation since the capital no‑fly zone policy.

【協創資料：不存在“通道化自採自銷”或損害公司及股東利益情形】藍鯨新聞10月13日電，有投資者問，今日，媒體報道對貴司提出三點質疑：322億元伺服器採購公告未見對應算力銷售合同；向淨資產不足20萬元、參保1人的麥塔倍斯預計8.5億元關聯銷售（半年已發生1.34億元）；向啟朔科技3億元關聯採購。市場據此懷疑交易呈＂通道化自採自銷＂。協創資料在互動平臺表示，公司伺服器採購主要用於雲算力服務，採購規模根據客戶訂單、專案規劃、資金安排及交付節奏審慎確定，屬於算力基礎設施建設的前置投入；採購預計額度與實際銷售合同不存在一一對應關係，不能據此推導。公司與麥塔倍斯、啟朔科技之間的交易均基於真實經營需要，以市場價格為定價原則，並已履行關聯交易審議、關聯董事迴避及披露程式；截至2026年6月30日，向麥塔倍斯銷售算力服務等累計發生13,412.36萬元，向啟朔科技採購伺服器累計發生9,377.19萬元，均未超過獲批額度，交易價格與市場參考價格不存在較大差異。公司不存在應披露而未披露事項，不存在所謂“通道化自採自銷”或損害公司及股東利益的情形。對於與事實不符的資訊，公司將依法採取必要措施維護公司及投資者合法權益。

### 2026-10-13 21:44:10  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Currency #Importance 0

South African Rand Firmer — The South African rand rose to just below 16.5 per US dollar, near its highest since late September, mainly supported by a softer US dollar and lower oil prices. Meanwhile, attention remained focused on geopolitical developments, the inflation outlook and the central banks' moves. The South African Reserve Bank (SARB) recently cautioned that second-round effects could cause higher fuel and transport costs to feed through to broader prices, wages and inflation expectations. The risk of a severe El Niño event have added to upside risks, particularly for imported and food inflation. Fuel prices surged to record highs following the October 7 adjustment, surpassing previous peaks recorded in June and complicating the central bank’s efforts to bring inflation back to its 3% target. The central bank raised the rates by 25 bps to 7.25% last month, while markets are pricing in two more increases over the next six months.

### 2026-10-13 20:52:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Saudi Arabia #Crude Oil Production #Importance 1

Saudi Arabia Crude Oil Production Recovers in September — Saudi Arabia’s crude oil production rebounded by 2.134 million barrels per day in September 2026, reaching 8.372 million barrels per day after falling to its lowest level in more than three decades. Despite the recovery, output remains well below pre-conflict levels. Oil exports across the Middle East also moved closer to normal last month, as regional producers increased shipments through the Strait of Hormuz, a key route for global energy supplies. Saudi Arabia further improved its export capacity by restoring flows through a major pipeline that diverts crude to the Red Sea, providing an alternative to the Strait of Hormuz. The pipeline had been targeted by Yemen’s Houthi rebels during the conflict. However, the durability of the recovery remains uncertain. Recent Iranian attacks on tankers passing through the strait, alongside escalating Houthi hostilities, have renewed concerns over shipping security and the risk of further disruptions to regional oil supplies.

### 2026-10-13 20:47:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Current Account #Importance 1

German Current Account Surplus Narrows Slightly in August — Germany’s current account surplus edged down to €10.3 billion in August 2026 from a revised €10.9 billion in the same month last year, as wider deficits in services and secondary income outweighed improvements in the goods and primary income balances. The services deficit widened to €11.5 billion from €9.9 billion, while the secondary income deficit increased to €6.8 billion from €6.0 billion. Meanwhile, the goods surplus rose to €13.1 billion from €12.4 billion a year earlier. Exports increased by 7.1% year-on-year to €106.2 billion, while imports grew by 7.4% to €93.2 billion. The primary income surplus also expanded to €15.6 billion from €14.4 billion. Despite the slight year-on-year decline in August, Germany’s cumulative current account surplus for the first eight months of 2026 increased to €140.4 billion, up from €133.7 billion in the corresponding period of 2025.

Oman Inflation Rate Edges Lower in September — Oman’s annual inflation rate eased to 3.2% in September 2026, down from 3.4% in August. Inflation slowed sharply in transport, falling to 4.8% from 8.5% in the previous month. Inflation remained at 2.2% for education, 1.7% for health, 0.4% for culture and entertainment, 0.1% for clothing and footwear, and 3.6% for restaurants and hotels. In contrast, price growth accelerated for food to 8% from 7% and furnishings to 3.3% from 3.1%, while housing and utilities prices edged up 0.1%, reversing a 0.6% decline in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 0.5% in September, following a 0.3% decline in the previous month.

### 2026-10-13 18:19:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Portugal Inflation Rate Confirmed at 3-Year High — Portugal’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.6% in September 2026, marking the highest reading since August 2023, from 3.3% in August 2026, confirming the preliminary estimate. The increase was mainly driven by higher fuel costs, with energy prices up 15.3% from 12.2% in August. Transport prices were the main upward contributor (8.2% vs 6.4%), followed by restaurants and accommodation services (7.2% vs 6.2%). Unprocessed food prices also picked up to 3.7% from 3.4%. Core inflation, which excludes energy and unprocessed food, rose to 2.7% from 2.6%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 1.2% after no change in August, as clothing prices jumped 21.5% with the start of the autumn/winter season. Meanwhile, harmonised inflation held at 3.6%, well above the ECB’s 2% target.

The growth in the national index surpassed that of Dublin, which saw residential property prices rise by 3.9%, amid a soft increase in house prices (3.3%) and a larger increase in apartment prices (5.3%). ### 2026-10-13 18:05:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #NFIB Business Optimism Index #Importance 2

US Small Business Sentiment Deteriorates Further — The US NFIB Small Business Optimism Index fell to 97.9 in September 2026, the lowest in three months, from 98.7 in August and roughly in line with forecasts of 98. Of the 10 Index components, three rose, six fell, and one stayed unchanged. The Index declined mainly as businesses became less optimistic about future conditions and fewer firms considered it a good time to expand. Reports of labor quality or availability as owners’ single most important problem rose 3 points to 26%, making it the top reported issue for the third consecutive month. The second top issue was inflation, with 20% of owners reporting it as their single most important problem, up 4 points from August. "Small business owners are proceeding with caution as they interpret how rising costs, interest rates, and the difficulty in finding qualified employees are impacting their business”, said NFIB Chief Economist Bill Dunkelberg.

South Africa Mining Sector Plunges Further in August — Mining production in South Africa plummeted by 12.7% year-on-year in August 2026, following a revised 7% decline in the prior month. This marked the fourth consecutive month of downturn and the steepest annual drop since October 2022, primarily due to reduced output from PGMs (-22.5%); iron ore (-16.2%) and coal (-11.7%). Significant declines were also recorded for diamonds (-41.1%); nickel (-32.2%); other metallic minerals (-12.5%); other non-metallic minerals (-11.2%); copper (-7.3%) and gold (-5.7% ). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, mining production shrank by 5.4%, after decreasing by 1.9% in the previous month. Mining production slumped by 5.2% in the three months ended August 2026 compared with the previous three months.

EU Natural Gas Prices Rise Toward 4-Year High — European natural gas prices climbed to €86 per megawatt-hour on Tuesday, reaching their highest level since December 2022 as concerns over limited supplies and low storage levels intensified ahead of winter. Gas facilities across Europe are currently 73.75% full, leaving inventories well below typical levels for this time of year. Forecasts for colder weather in northwestern Europe from next weekend could further increase heating demand, putting additional pressure on already limited reserves. With storage levels lower than in previous years, the market remains particularly vulnerable to sudden temperature changes or unexpected supply disruptions. Stronger European demand could also intensify competition for liquefied natural gas (LNG) cargoes in the global market. Meanwhile, escalating tensions in the Middle East have driven North Asian LNG prices to nearly three times their pre-conflict levels, increasing competition between Asian and European buyers for available shipments.

FTSE MIB Gains for Third Day — The FTSE MIB rose for a third straight session to above 50,200 on Tuesday, driven by gains in banking and utility stocks amid softer oil prices and bond yields. Financials moved mostly higher as investors monitored Italy’s banking consolidation battle. BPER Banca gained 1.6%, while Monte dei Paschi di Siena rose 1.1%, UniCredit added 1.0% and Intesa Sanpaolo advanced 0.8%, despite MPS reiterating concerns over Intesa’s revised takeover offer. Poste Italiane climbed 1.2% after Morgan Stanley resumed coverage with an overweight rating. Utilities also performed well, led by Enel (1.4%), Terna (1.2%) and A2A (1.1%). On the downside, technology stocks underperformed, with STMicroelectronics falling 3.1%. Stellantis traded little changed after reporting a 1% year-on-year decline in estimated Q3 shipments. Investors also monitored the government’s 2027 budget plan, as concerns over projected deficits could complicate Italy’s efforts to exit the EU’s excessive deficit procedure.

【貨機衝出跑道墜海致兩人身亡，香港公佈調查進展】據香港特區政府網新聞公報，民航意外調查機構就2025年10月20日一宗涉及ACT航空公司（代表阿聯酋航空貨運公司經營）一架波音747-481 BDSF型貨機（登記標誌TC-ACF）在香港國際機場偏離跑道，導致一輛停放在跑道範圍外的地面機場保安車輛內兩名機場保安人員死亡的意外，發表調查臨時宣告。調查小組已完成主要調查工作，包括回收殘骸，以及收集來自航空公司、氣象服務、航空交通管制、機場等的相關資料和紀錄。相關飛機部件（包括機上記錄系統）已送往製造國進行檢驗。民航意外調查機構亦安排調查人員前往海外，在當地監督重要檢驗工作。調查的其中一個重點是第4發動機推力杆向前移動，以致飛機在著陸滑行過程中向左偏轉並偏離跑道。截至目前的相關飛機部件技術檢驗中，並未發現與意外相關的機件故障。

French Stocks Rise for 3rd Straight Session — The CAC 40 rose 0.4% to 7,860 on Tuesday, extending gains for a third consecutive session as lower oil prices and bond yields supported sentiment, helping offset growing concerns over AI valuations. Investors increasingly doubt the return-on-investment timelines for massive corporate capital expenditures in AI amid elevated borrowing costs. Gains were led by Société Générale (1.8%), L’Oréal (1.3%), Danone (1.3%), Orange (1.3%), and EssilorLuxottica (1.2%). In contrast, semiconductor giant STMicroelectronics bore the brunt of a broader tech retreat, falling 4.7%. Meanwhile, LVMH fell 1.0% after reporting weaker-than-expected third-quarter organic sales, weighed down by subdued demand in China. Hermès (-0.8%) and Kering (-0.2%) also fell, as LVMH’s performance, widely viewed as a bellwether for the luxury sector, fueled doubts about the strength of the broader recovery.

【Japanese-linked tankers resume direct Middle East loadings; Gulf shipping risk eases but freight pressure remains high】Japanese-linked tankers have begun a cautious return to direct carriage of Middle East crude for the first time since fighting began in February, though freight and logistics costs remain elevated. Over the past seven months risk-averse Japanese refiners and shipowners largely avoided Gulf routes, using long-distance ship-to-ship transfers off India and Southeast Asia to limit exposure—increasing seller dependence and forgoing higher freight margins. Vessel-tracking shows Idemitsu Kosan’s VLCC Idemitsu Maru left the Gulf in April and arrived in Japan earlier this month with a full cargo of Oman crude loaded at Mina al Fahal. Shipbrokers say at least two other Japanese-controlled VLCCs are expected to call Fujairah in the coming days to load crude bound for Japan.

王毅請哈勒敦轉達習近平主席對穆罕默德總統的親切問候，表示中阿兩國是相互信任的好朋友，在習近平主席和穆罕默德總統戰略引領下，中阿政治互信強，合作成果實，兩國關係已超越雙邊範疇，成為全球南方國家攜手發展振興的典範。今年4月，阿布扎比王儲哈立德殿下對中國進行成功訪問，習近平主席和李強總理分別同王儲殿下會晤，延續了兩國密切的高層交往，也彰顯了中阿關係的戰略屬性。不管國際地區形勢如何變化，中方願繼續同阿方一道，在兩國元首指引下，推動雙邊關係不斷邁上新臺階。

王毅表示，當前中東格局深刻演變，衝突在多層面延宕，外溢影響不斷衝擊地區安全和發展。要和平、不要戰爭，要對話、不要對抗，要公道、不要霸道，日益成為地區各國人民的普遍追求和共同期盼。和平之路從來不是坦途，促和止戰需要凝聚合力。我們相信，只要各方堅持和平選擇，保持和平定力，就一定能夠迎來中東地區光明前景。中國始終是中東和平的建設者、穩定的促進者、發展的貢獻者。我們將秉持習近平主席提出的一系列致力於和平穩定的倡議和主張，為在中東地區實現全面停火，推進政治解決，儘早重啟談判，促成持久和平，繼續作出中國貢獻，發揮中國的建設性作用。

哈勒敦請王毅轉達穆罕默德總統對習近平主席的誠摯問候，祝賀新中國成立77週年，感謝中方對哈立德王儲訪華的熱情款待，期待年內開展元首層面的外交。哈勒敦介紹了當前海灣和中東局勢以及阿方所做的工作，表示願同中方加強溝通協調，共同促進地區和平穩定。中國始終是阿方值得信賴和依靠的夥伴，希推動兩國全面戰略伙伴關係邁上新的臺階。

雙方還就共同關心的問題深入交換了意見。

【Three ministries target doubling of China inland new‑energy vessel fleet by 2028】The Ministry of Transport and two other departments issued the Inland New‑Energy Vessels Promotion and Application Action Plan (2026–2030). By 2028 the inland new‑energy vessel fleet should double versus 2025: electric transport vessels to exceed 1,000 and methanol‑powered vessels to exceed 100, with significant increases in electric government and port service vessels and initial full‑scenario demonstrations. Yangtze trunkline methanol refuelling capability is to achieve end‑to‑end connectivity and charging/battery‑swap facilities in the Yangtze River Delta water network to be largely networked. Designated green shipping corridors on the Yangtze, Xijiang trunk, the Beijing–Hangzhou Grand Canal and Yangtze Delta electrification pilot zones should form about 3,000 km of zero‑carbon waterway transport routes.

### 2026-10-13 15:29:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Czechia Inflation Confirmed at 5-Month High — The annual inflation rate in the Czech Republic rose to 2.5% in September 2026 from 1.9% in the previous month. This marked the highest reading since April, driven by an acceleration in transport costs (13.2% vs. 9.6% in August), with fuel and lubricant prices surging by 37.5% year-on-year, and a moderation in the deflation of food and non-alcoholic beverages (-3.5% vs. -4.3%). Prices also rose faster for housing and utilities (1.7% vs. 1.5%), recreation and culture (2.5% vs. 0.4%), personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (4.0% vs. 3.8%), information and communication (2.9% vs. 2.0%), and education (4.7% vs. 4.0%). Meanwhile, inflation eased for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (4.6% vs. 4.7%), health (3.8% vs. 4.2%), and restaurants and accommodation services (4.2% vs. 4.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices were unchanged in September, down from a 0.3% increase in August.

India 10Y Yield Eases From 3-Year High — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec hovered around 7.37%, easing from three-year highs as investors stepped in to seek value after a sharp selloff, with heavy purchases by local lenders, particularly state-run banks, helping support prices. However, gains remained limited ahead of INR 312 billion in state debt sales and the RBI’s INR 250 billion open market bond sale, while another INR 340 billion auction is scheduled for Friday. Yields have risen 20 basis points over four sessions since the RBI raised its policy rate by 25 basis points and shifted to a calibrated tightening stance. Expectations of further rate hikes strengthened after September inflation accelerated to 5.54%, while Brent crude remained above $105 a barrel. Investors also awaited US inflation data for clues on the Federal Reserve’s policy outlook.

### 2026-10-13 15:26:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Construction Output #Importance 1

Slovakia Construction Output Falls at Fastest Pace in Nearly 2 Years — Slovakia’s construction output fell by 4.8% year-on-year in August 2026, reversing a 2.3% increase in the previous month. This marked the second annual decline this year, after January, and the steepest drop since September 2024. The result for the second month of the summer holiday period reflected a decline in domestic construction (-3.0% vs 4.7% in July), with decreases in the dominant new construction activity (-0.7% vs 4.9%), repair and maintenance of buildings (-6.4% vs 5.0%), and civil engineering works (-10.3% vs 1.1%). At the same time, growth in building construction slowed sharply to 0.6% from 6.5%. Construction activity abroad also declined further, falling by 16.0%, the largest decrease since January, after a 13.5% drop in July. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, construction output dropped by 4.9%. In the first eight months of 2026, construction activity was 6.3% higher than in the same period last year.

Iron Ore Extends Decline on Ample Supply — Iron ore futures fell toward CNY 680 per ton, approaching four-year lows as abundant global supply, elevated Chinese inventories and falling shipping costs continued to pressure prices. Global mining capacity is expanding, while producers are boosting shipments in the fourth quarter to meet quarterly and annual targets. Iron ore shipments and arrivals at Chinese ports have also remained elevated, supported by a significant increase in volumes from smaller mining operations. Meanwhile, freight costs continued to ease, with industry data showing the Capesize C5 route from Australia to China declining by $0.64 per ton and the C3 route from Brazil to China falling by $0.37 per ton on Monday. Adding to the bearish outlook, the World Steel Association projected global steel demand would contract by 0.1% in 2026, reversing its April forecast for 0.3% growth amid weakening demand in China and disruptions stemming from the US-Iran conflict.

### 2026-10-13 15:20:45  #公司 #市場 #國際

【敏華控股：受成本端衝擊以及美國業務拖累，預計中期溢利同比減少70%至90%】10月13日，敏華控股（01999.HK）釋出中期（3月至9月）盈利警告，期內，本公司權益擁有人應占溢利將較上年同期的11.46億港元減少約70%至90%。公告稱，根據本集團目前可得的資料，本集團的主營收益與去年同期相比保持穩定，但上述溢利的下降主要歸因於兩方面的原因：一是，自中東衝突爆發以來，化工類的核心原材料價格持續大幅上行，推高集團產品生產成本。與此同時，行業內卷加劇，產品售價調整存在滯後性，未能完全覆蓋原材料成本增幅，本集團整體毛利率有所下滑。雖然主營收益基本維持平穩，溢利主要受成本端衝擊。二是，因為原材料成本上漲及市場需求疲弱，2025年12月收購的美國業務產生營運虧損，並且被收購公司的直接全資附屬公司Southern Motion已在美國申請進行第11章破產程式，該項收購相關的商譽及其他無形資產於期間內確認減值虧損。

### 2026-10-13 15:18:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #World #Full Year GDP Growth #Importance 3

IMF Upgrades 2026 Global Growth Forecast — The IMF raised its 2026 global growth forecast by 0.1 percentage points to 3.1%, with growth projected to rebound to 3.4% in 2027, according to its October 2026 World Economic Outlook. Resilient advanced economies and increased AI investment underpin the upgrade, although growth is expected to slow from 3.5% in 2025 as high energy prices linked to the US–Iran war pose significant risks, particularly for low-income countries and energy-importing economies. US growth is forecast at 2.3% in both 2026 and 2027, while China’s expansion is expected to slow to 4.5% and 4.0%, respectively, largely due to weak domestic demand. Japan’s economy is forecast to expand by 0.7% in both years, while India is projected to grow by 7.0% annually over the same period. Eurozone growth is expected to ease to 1.1% this year before recovering to 1.2% in 2027, amid high energy costs and weak consumer confidence. The UK’s 2026 forecast was raised to 1.3%, while its 2027 outlook was lowered to 1.2%.

Turkey Current Account Surplus Narrows — Turkey’s current account surplus narrowed to $4.05 billion in August 2026 from $4.62 billion a year earlier. The goods trade deficit widened to $3.75 billion from $2.80 billion, as imports ($27.07 billion) exceeded exports ($23.33 billion). Meanwhile, the services surplus increased to $9.71 billion from $9.34 billion. The primary income deficit edged down to $1.89 billion from $1.90 billion, while the secondary income deficit slightly fell to $18 million from $21 million. In the first eight months of 2026, Turkey’s current account deficit widened to $31.50 billion from $19.67 billion in the corresponding period a year earlier.

### 2026-10-13 15:08:38  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【Liaoning to open rural collective operational construction land to market】Liaoning provincial government unveiled measures to remove factor-flow barriers and activate stock land. The province will build a secondary land market, attract a range of market participants, support rural industrial land demand, repurpose idle farmhouses and orderly allow rural collective operational construction land to enter the market to improve urban–rural land allocation efficiency and intensify land use. Natural resources authorities will establish a land-review coordination mechanism to prioritise reasonable land for leading industries and major projects. Financial measures include annually recommending more than 100 projects to financial institutions and guiding early financial involvement during feasibility for projects with total investment above 1 bln yuan to jointly design financing. Technology support will back innovation consortia of firms, universities and research institutes with grants up to 10 mln yuan, capped at 30% of R&D spend, to tackle key industrial core technologies.

Shanghai Composite Index rose 0.10%, Shenzhen Composite Index fell 0.25% and the ChiNext Index closed down 0.18% after trading up more than 1% intraday. Electronic components stocks drove gains, with Xunjiexing and Jinbaize both hitting the 20% daily limit and firms including Chaosheng Electronics, Zhongjing Electronics and Aohong Electronics also limit-up. Defense and marine-equipment names rallied in the afternoon; Hangfa Technology hit limit-up and Jianglong Shipbuilding rallied over 9%. Lagging sectors included insurance, batteries, wind-power equipment, CROs, innovative drugs and energy metals. Combined turnover on Shanghai and Shenzhen reached 1.67 tln yuan, about 138.1 bln yuan higher than the prior session. Around 2,800 stocks advanced and about 2,300 declined.

### 2026-10-13 15:02:21  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Stock Market #Importance 1

Japan’s Nikkei Slides on Mounting AI Concerns — The Nikkei 225 Index fell 1.82% to close at 67,777 on Tuesday, extending its decline to a fourth consecutive session and following an overnight tech-led selloff on Wall Street. The downturn reflected growing concerns over the AI trade, as investors reassessed capital spending prospects amid rising borrowing costs. Market sentiment was further pressured by intensifying competition from China’s ChangXin Memory Technologies (CXMT), which reported progress in developing a new chip architecture. Broadcom CEO Hock Tan remarked that the AI boom had attracted investors whose strategies resembled gambling, while billionaire Ray Dalio described AI as a “classic bubble” nearing a potential burst as interest rates rise and investors seek to convert wealth into cash. Technology and AI-related stocks recorded substantial losses, with Kioxia Holdings plunging 13.3%, SoftBank Group dropping 8.5%, Tokyo Electron falling 7.2%, Taiyo Yuden declining 4.9% and Advantest losing 2.9%.

【KB Securities: Korean chip sell-off may reflect investor doubts over earnings sustainability】KB Securities analysts E.T. Lee and D.E. Lee said that despite AI-driven elevated profit expectations, the recent pullback in Korean semiconductor stocks may partly reflect investor skepticism about the sustainability of earnings. Samsung Electronics and SK Hynix fell for a fifth straight session. The analysts said investors continue to view semiconductors as cyclical: supply bottlenecks can sharply lift profits in booms, but their easing can lead to inventory gluts and overinvestment. They point to similar dynamics in post-pandemic diagnostics, masks, toilet paper and container shipping.

### 2026-10-13 14:50:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Construction Output #Importance 1

Hungary Construction Output Jumps to 4-Year High — Hungary’s construction output jumped 21.9% year-on-year in August 2026, rebounding sharply from a 12.2% decline in the previous month. The latest figure marked the largest increase since February 2022, as both major construction groups recorded stronger activity. Civil engineering output surged 102.4%, reversing a 14.0% plunge, driven largely by a 183.6% increase in output in the relevant segment, a 142% jump in road and railway construction, and a 319.9% rise in utility projects. Meanwhile, building construction declined at a softer pace, falling 9.6% compared with a 10.7% drop in July. On a seasonally and working-day-adjusted monthly basis, construction output increased 14.0% in August, following a 4.6% decline in the previous month. In the January–August period, total construction output edged up 0.1% compared with the same period a year earlier.

【硫磺供給緊張加劇，全球糧食價格承壓】霍爾木茲海峽航運受阻疊加伊朗戰事，全球硫磺供應大幅收緊。硫磺作為油氣行業副產品，是生產硫酸、磷肥與鍊銅的核心原料，當前價格已飆升至接近每噸1000美元，較戰前漲近十倍。資金實力更強的礦企在硫磺競價中壓過低毛利化肥廠商，化肥企業被迫減產。美盛、摩洛哥OCP等化肥巨頭表示，難以將原料成本完全轉嫁給農戶，警告後續全球農作物單產將受衝擊，推高食品價格。大宗商品報價機構Argus分析師拉加夫・賈恩表示，在這場稀缺資源競價戰中，利潤率較低的化肥行業，處於下風。機構Kpler提示，霍爾木茲海峽船舶滯留問題仍在惡化，硫磺貿易擾動持續。硫磺同時作用於工業製造與農業生產，供應鏈緊張不僅影響電子產品、電網產業鏈，明年麵包等食品價格也或將上漲。

### 2026-10-13 14:24:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Romania Inflation Edges Higher in September — The annual inflation rate in Romania rose to 6.6% in September 2026 from 6.2% in the previous month, above market expectations of 6.45%. The acceleration was driven by faster price growth in food goods (3.0% vs. 2.6%) and non-food goods (7.1% vs. 6.4%). Among food items, prices increased for bread and bakery products (4.8%), milk and dairy products (4.6%), and meat and meat products (4.1%). Fuel prices remained a key contributor to inflation, with petrol and diesel prices surging by 28.4% and 37.6%, respectively. Meanwhile, services inflation eased slightly to 11.1% from 11.3% in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices grew by 0.77% in August, accelerating slightly from 0.22% increase in the preceding period. Meanwhile, the HICP-based annual inflation rate rose to 6.5% from 6.3% in August.

【高盛：上調比亞迪電子目標價至22港元，2027年收入增長將復甦】高盛發表研報，比亞迪電子2027年收入增長將復甦，受惠於在儲存器成本相對穩定下，智慧手機及個人計算機需求企穩，以及公司產品組合升級；來自蘋果及比亞迪的收入敞口增加，並擴充套件至AI伺服器供應鏈(液冷、供電)。該行繼續預期比亞迪電子毛利率至2028年將復甦，以2027年目標市盈率10倍計，目標價由21港元上調至22港元，維持“沽售”評級。高盛表示，終端需求在高儲存器成本下偏溫和，同時已把AI運算基礎設施納入預測，反映公司向AI資料中心擴張，並下調消費電子收入預測，相應將比亞迪電子2026至2028年淨利潤預測相應分別下調40%、3%及32%，仍預期2026年下半年按半年計可增長24%，因儲存器成本企穩可帶動電動車及消費電子終端需求改善，加上季節性較佳及更多旗艦機型推出。

### 2026-10-13 14:05:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

German Inflation Confirmed at Near 3-Year High — Germany’s annual inflation rate accelerated to 3.3% in September 2026, confirming preliminary estimates, up from 2.9% in August. It was the highest inflation rate since December 2023, mainly driven by a renewed surge in energy inflation, which rose to 14.9%, its highest level since February 2023, amid stalled US-Iran negotiations and higher crude oil prices. Energy prices rose for the third consecutive month, mainly driven by increases in motor fuel prices (37.1% vs 27.7% in August) and heating oil prices (74.9% vs 49.6%). Food inflation also increased to 0.4% from 0.1%, with egg prices rising 15.2% and fresh vegetable prices climbing 12%, while services inflation slowed to 2.7% from 2.8%. Core inflation, excluding energy and unprocessed food, held steady at 2.4%. The EU-harmonized inflation rate also rose to 3.3%, its highest level since late 2023 and well above the ECB’s 2% target. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.6% in September, accelerating from a 0.2% gain in August.

### 2026-10-13 14:04:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Remains Under Pressure from Ample Supply — Nickel traded around $15,600 per tonne, remaining near a recent over nine-month low of $15,510, as persistent oversupply and a stronger US dollar weighed on prices. LME inventories remained elevated at around 283,000 tonnes in early October, underscoring ample availability despite some production disruptions, while Indonesian nickel ore benchmarks had declined and premiums turned negative. Additionally, the dollar index had recently reached its highest level since April 2025, while rising expectations of a December Federal Reserve rate hike added pressure on dollar-denominated metals. Overnight declines in Chinese nickel and stainless steel prices also pointed to continued weakness across the metals market. Meanwhile, Indonesia is considering a moratorium on new smelters producing semi-finished nickel products to address an estimated global surplus of 2.5 million tonnes. If implemented, the restrictions could curb future capacity growth.

### 2026-10-13 14:03:50  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent #DXY #Impact mixed

【Goldman Sachs: Russia diesel release limited; European strategic reserve release is key variable】Goldman Sachs says planned Russian diesel releases would have limited global price impact, while a potential 50 million-barrel release from European strategic reserves could cut wholesale diesel prices on the US Gulf Coast and in Europe by about $11/bbl. Analyst Eamonn Sheridan says diesel remains the most stressed segment of the Iran-war energy shock and that relief depends on government inventory releases rather than additional Russian supply. Goldman’s simulation shows a European reserve release would deliver meaningful but temporary relief, narrowing refining crack spreads and easing transport and industrial cost pass-through to inflation. The bank’s broader view is product markets stay tight through 2027, implying any diesel softening is likely transient, crack spreads and refiners’ margins will remain elevated, and persistent diesel tightness will keep refinery runs high, supporting crude even if wider supply concerns ease.

【SAMR seeks public comment on draft notice to optimize environmental electricity price regulation】The State Administration for Market Regulation (SAMR) published a draft notice seeking public comment that would link environmental electricity-price payments to hourly emissions compliance for coal-fired generators. Under the proposal, grid companies would directly deduct payments for desulfurization, denitrification, dust removal and ultra-low-emission charges from settlements for hours when a coal-fired unit’s hourly average SO2, NOx or particulate concentrations exceed regulatory limits; the rule covers both public and captive coal-fired units’ output delivered to the grid.

### 2026-10-13 13:58:29  #市場

【節後公募發行回暖，本週65只新基金開啟認購，環比大增逾209%】國慶長假後，公募基金髮行市場迅速回暖。公募排排網資料顯示，按認購起始日統計，本週（10月12日—10月18日）全市場共計有65只新基金開啟認購，較上週環比增長209.52%，並創下近6周發行新高。融智投資FOF基金經理李春瑜表示，國慶長假後，基金髮行市場迅速回暖的原因包括：一是市場預期修復，公募機構看好四季度A股結構性機會，選擇節後集中上新，把握低位建倉視窗。二是資金端存在配置需求，長假後居民資金迴流，理財資金尋求多元資產佈局，承接新發產品。三是產品供給豐富，發行陣容包含權益、FOF、固收+等多型別產品，適配不同風險偏好資金，助推發行熱度。權益類基金成為本週發行主力。65只新發基金中，權益基金佔36只，包括19只股票型基金和17只偏股混合型基金，合計佔比55.38%。(新華財經)

ASX 200 Closes Steady — The ASX 200 ended flat at 8,784 on Tuesday after two sessions of gains, as strength in energy minerals, consumer services, and financials was offset by weakness in tech, consumer non-durables, and industrials. Traders digested data showing Australia's business mood fell to a four-month low in September, marking the 7th straight negative reading amid oil volatility as Middle East tensions persisted. Minutes from the RBA’s September meeting signaled further hikes remained possible, with some upside risks flagged in August materialising. Futures tied to the S&P 500 and Nasdaq slipped ahead of bank earnings, Fed officials' remarks, and key inflation reports. The big four banks rallied 1.3%–2.3%, while AGL Energy jumped 4.1% and Ampol Ltd. rose 1.6%. On the downside, heavyweight BHP fell 1.8%, along with Fortescue (-2.9%), South32 (-3.6%), and Pro Medicus (-1.6%). Qantas slid 2.9% after ending its domestic codeshare with Air NZ, with the partnership set to scale back from April 7, 2027.

Finland Current Account Shifts to Deficit — Finland’s current account swung to a EUR 890 million deficit in August 2026 from a EUR 319 million surplus in the corresponding month of the previous year. The deterioration was mainly driven by a shift in the primary income balance to a EUR 566 million deficit from a EUR 447 million surplus, while the secondary income deficit widened to EUR 174 million from EUR 124 million. The goods trade surplus narrowed to EUR 143 million from EUR 168 million a year earlier, as goods exports rose 24% year-on-year to EUR 7.2 billion, while imports increased at a faster 26% to EUR 7.1 billion. Meanwhile, the services deficit narrowed to EUR 292 million from EUR 171 million. The 12-month moving average of the current account remained in deficit by EUR 700 million. Separately, net capital flows from Finland to abroad reached EUR 5.8 billion, largely reflecting outflows of EUR 4.9 billion in other investments.

### 2026-10-13 12:57:18  #其他

【英國機構呼籲加強前沿AI安全管理】英國人工智慧安全研究所12日發表文章說，隨著前沿人工智慧（AI）能力快速提升，安全風險正前移至模型開發階段，而現有安全評估方法難以跟上技術發展，有必要探索新的技術安全實踐，以確保前沿AI開發活動安全進行。文章指出，此前AI模型安全評估主要著眼於確保公開發布時的安全性，但如今部分AI系統自主能力增強，尤其在網路安全領域，即使模型尚未公開發布，也可能在訓練、內部部署和評估過程中帶來風險。人工智慧安全研究所提出八項技術安全實踐重點，包括開發全過程風險評估與記錄、AI系統全生命週期監督、維護和評估監督機制、AI智慧體安全事件響應和報告、共享有關AI發展速度的資訊、獨立審查與評估、安全相關領域能力評估以及對AI對齊方面情況的評估。文章說，該機構已與AI行業合作，在超過55個AI模型公開發布前對其進行了獨立評估，制定了安全保障評估原則，幫助建設風險評估和安全框架。隨著這些實踐逐步成熟，需要透過相關國際標準制定機構將其轉化為正式技術標準。（新華社）

### 2026-10-13 12:53:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Netherlands #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Netherlands September Inflation Confirmed at 3.4% — The annual inflation rate in the Netherlands was confirmed at a four-month high of 3.4% in September 2026, slightly picking up from 3.3% in August. Price growth accelerated mainly in transport (9.3% vs. 8.3%in August), driven by surging motor fuel prices (27.2%), with diesel recording the sharpest increase. Diesel averaged €2.50 per liter during the month. Inflation also accelerated for housing and utilities (4.2% vs. 4.1%), health (1.3% vs. 0.8%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.3% vs. 1.9%), and recreation and culture (3.0% vs. 2.2%). Meanwhile, prices fell further for food and non-alcoholic beverages (-1.8% vs. -1.0%), while inflation eased for clothing and footwear (0.6% vs. 1.1%). Monthly consumer prices rose 0.1%, the smallest increase in three periods, following a 0.3% gain in August. Meanwhile, annual HICP inflation, which excludes owner-occupied housing costs, rose to 3.0% from 2.8% in August.

【魏懷仁、陳大衛、廖景芳被判死刑，緬北魏家犯罪集團案一審宣判】10月13日，福建省泉州市中級人民法院和南安市人民法院一審公開宣判魏家犯罪集團系列案。對魏懷仁、陳大衛、廖景芳3名被告人判處死刑；對魏青濤、康敏、熊恆星3名被告人判處無期徒刑，對蘇紅相、鄧子達、陳龍江等55名被告人分別判處二十四年至三年三個月不等有期徒刑，並相應判處罰金、沒收財產、驅逐出境等附加刑。經審理查明，2019年以來，以魏懷仁、魏青松（另案處理）等為首要分子，親信下屬陳龍江及武裝力量頭目茶貴成、顏龍帝（均另案處理）等為重要成員的魏懷仁犯罪集團，和以魏榕、魏青海（均另案處理）、魏青濤為首要分子，家族成員蘇紅相及親信下屬鄧子達等為重要成員的亨利犯罪集團，利用魏家家族在緬甸果敢地區的影響力，發展和依託魏家家族所掌控的武裝力量，在果敢老街設立多個犯罪園區，招攬、吸引多名“金主”入駐並提供武裝庇護，自行或夥同“金主”實施電信網路詐騙、開設賭場、敲詐勒索、組織賣淫、販賣毒品、組織他人偷越國（邊）境等犯罪，涉賭、詐等資金122億餘元。魏家犯罪集團還夥同園區電信網路詐騙團伙殺害不服從管理的涉詐人員，造成2名中國公民死亡。此外，魏家家族成員陳大衛，利用其掌控的武裝力量肆意殺人，造成2名中國公民死亡。泉州市中級人民法院和南安市人民法院認為，被告人魏懷仁、魏青濤等人組織、領導、參加犯罪集團實施犯罪，其行為分別構成故意殺人罪、詐騙罪等7項罪名。法庭根據各被告人犯罪的事實、性質、情節和對於社會的危害程度，依法作出上述判決。人大代表、政協委員、當事人家屬和各界群眾旁聽了宣判。（央視新聞）

【農業農村部設立我國首個布氏鯨重要棲息地】農業農村部今天（13日）釋出公告，設立廣西北海潿洲島布氏鯨重要棲息地，填補我國近海大型鯨類棲息生境保護的空白，為維護海洋生物多樣性保護、保護海洋漁業生態環境樹立了重要標杆。此次設立的北海潿洲島布氏鯨重要棲息地海域面積達335.43平方公里，禁漁期長達8.5個月，包括每年12月至次年4月專項禁漁，疊加每年5月1日至8月16日南海伏季休漁。布氏鯨是全球洄游性海洋物種，為國家一級保護野生動物。每年12月至次年4月，我國北海潿洲島海域餌料豐富、生境優良，大批布氏鯨如期洄游至該海域覓食棲息、生長發育。自2018年監測以來，潿洲島海域已累計識別超過70頭個體，形成結構穩定、活動規律清晰的野生種群。此次劃設布氏鯨重要棲息地，配套實施禁漁期制度，對保障個體存活、維繫種群可持續發展、開展全球布氏鯨協同保護具有重要作用。同時，能夠進一步強化棲息生境的專項保護，有效降低漁業作業、船舶航行、觀鯨旅遊等人為活動帶來的干擾，推動實現人與自然和諧共生。（央視新聞）

US Natural Gas Prices Edge Lower — US natural gas futures fell for a second day to $3.15 per MMBtu on Tuesday, as Gulf Coast producers restore operations after Hurricane Isaias, while warmer weather forecasts curbed early-season heating demand. As of Monday, around 25% of natural gas output remained shut in, according to the US Marine Minerals Administration. However, the impact on overall supply was limited, as offshore production accounted for just 2% of total marketed gas output. In addition, forecasts of above-average temperatures across the eastern US from October 22-26 could delay the seasonal pickup in heating demand. Prices also declined amid weaker LNG activity, with gas flows to the nine major export plants falling to 17.4 bcfd in October from 17.9 bcfd in September. Meanwhile, Lower 48 output dropped to 110.7 bcfd this month from record highs of 113.3 bcfd in August and September, amid hurricane-related shut-ins, force majeure events and pipeline disruptions in Kentucky, Texas, West Virginia and elsewhere.

### 2026-10-13 10:41:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #IMF #Calendar #Importance 1

IMF Edges Up Global 2026 Growth Forecast to 3.1% — The International Monetary Fund (IMF) raised its 2026 global growth forecast to 3.1%, up 0.1 percentage points from its July projection, boosted by strong investment in AI but partly offset by rising oil prices amid the Middle East conflict. The World Economic Outlook, released as global economic and financial leaders gather in Bangkok for the IMF and World Bank annual meetings, puts the 2026 global growth forecast below the 2025 growth rate of 3.5%, while keeping its 2027 forecast unchanged at 3.4%. Silvana Tenreyro, the IMF's chief economist, said that a prolonged conflict in the Middle East could put additional pressure on inflation and slow growth. A worsening of fiscal prospects, a reassessment of AI profitability, and extreme weather events could further weaken the outlook. The IMF held its US growth forecast at 2.3% for 2026, while slightly lowering its China growth forecast to 4.5% and raising its Japan and Eurozone growth forecasts to 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

Silver Pressured by Dollar Strength — Silver fell toward $60 an ounce on Tuesday, extending losses from the previous session as the US dollar climbed to an 18-month high ahead of key inflation reports that could shape the Federal Reserve’s next interest rate decision. The US consumer price index is due on Wednesday, followed by producer inflation data on Thursday. Markets are pricing in roughly an 80% probability that the Fed will leave interest rates unchanged this month, while assigning nearly an 88% chance of a rate hike in December. Investors also monitored geopolitical tensions as the US considers joining Saudi Arabia in its campaign against Iran-backed Houthi militants in Yemen, while the recently announced Russia-Ukraine “energy ceasefire” has already been violated. These developments have kept oil prices elevated, fueling inflation concerns and increasing expectations of further interest rate hikes.

Inflation Risks Prompt September Rate Hike: RBA Minutes — Minutes from the Reserve Bank of Australia’s September meeting showed that policymakers unanimously raised the cash rate by 25bps to 4.6%, citing persistent inflation risks. Several upside threats flagged in August had materialised, including Middle East tensions driving oil above forecasts, global cost pressures partly tied to AI demand, and stronger domestic activity. Inflation readings exceeded expectations, with businesses reporting widespread cost pass-throughs. Short-term expectations stayed high, raising concern about medium-term drift. While the labour market eased broadly as anticipated, demand remained firm and capacity pressures stronger than assessed. Members warned that delaying action risked entrenching inflation after a prolonged period above target. They also noted that holding steady could weaken the Australian dollar as other central banks tightened. The priority remained returning inflation to target, with forecasts pointing to a mid-2027 return to the midpoint.

### 2026-10-13 09:02:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Foreign Direct Investment #Importance 1

Philippines FDI Drops 44.1% in July — Net foreign direct investment (FDI) in the Philippines plunged by 44.1% year-on-year to USD 689 million in July 2026. The decline was driven by lower net inflows in net equity capital investments other than reinvestment of earnings and net debt instruments, dropping 55.5% and 49.1%, respectively, which more than offset the 39.9% increase in reinvestment of earnings. In the first seven months of the year, net FDI inflows decreased by 23.9% to USD 4.07 billion, reflecting lower net inflows across all major FDI components, such as net equity capital investments other than reinvestment of earnings (-6.9%), reinvestment of earnings (-14.2%), and net debt instruments (-30.6%).Capital placements came primarily from Japan, the United States, and Singapore. These funds were channeled largely into the manufacturing, financial and insurance, and real estate industries.

【摩根大通和高盛降低對AI雲初創公司Volta 50億美元貸款交易的風險敞口】隨著投資者對與人工智慧(AI)熱潮相關的高風險債務提出更高收益率要求，摩根大通和高盛在為AI初創公司Volta Infrastructure Holdings Ltd.安排50億美元貸款交易時，罕見地採取措施限制自身風險敞口。這兩家銀行正牽頭為新興AI雲公司Volta安排貸款融資。據知情人士透露，兩家銀行為Volta承銷了其中13億美元的定期貸款，而未承銷另外37億美元晶片貸款，旨在降低潛在風險。Volta的債務融資收益率約為11%，是目前AI融資領域流行的槓桿貸款市場中最高的借貸成本之一。值得注意的是，該交易未獲評級，這通常會讓此類債務的最大買家貸款抵押債券(CLO)卻步。知情人士還表示，為AI熱潮提供融資，尤其是幫助那些新成立或商業模式尚未經過市場檢驗的企業融資時，銀行往往需要在槓桿融資市場採用非常規的承銷結構。他們補充說，銀行正試圖規避潛在風險，確保不會陷入被動局面。

Japanese Shares Drop on Tech Selloff — The Nikkei 225 Index fell 2% to below 68,000 in post-holiday trading on Tuesday, extending its decline to a fourth consecutive session and following an overnight tech-led selloff on Wall Street. Technology stocks faced heavy selling pressure as concerns over the AI trade intensified, with investors reassessing capital spending prospects amid rising borrowing costs. Broadcom CEO Hock Tan said that the AI boom had attracted investors whose strategies resembled gambling, while billionaire Ray Dalio described AI as a “classic bubble” approaching a potential burst as interest rates rise and investors seek to convert wealth into cash. Significant losses were recorded among technology and AI-related stocks, including Kioxia Holdings (-9.3%), Advantest (-5.3%), SoftBank Group (-7%), Taiyo Yuden (-5.6%) and Tokyo Electron (-4.5%).

Australian Shares Inch Lower — Australian stocks slipped 21 points or 0.2% to 8,762 in early Tuesday trade, snapping two sessions of gains as U.S. stock futures weakened after a mixed Wall Street close overnight. Rising crude oil prices stoked inflation worries ahead of key U.S. CPI data this week. Locally, traders turned cautious ahead of September business confidence later today and upcoming labor market figures in the coming days. In main trading partner China, key data will also be released, including CPI/ PPI and trade performance, adding to the watchlist. Sectoral losses in non-energy minerals, tech, and consumer durables were partly offset by gains in energy minerals, financials, and retail trade. Heavyweights BHP Group slipped 1.6%, along with Fortescue (-2.1%), South32 (-3.3%), and Pro Medicus (-1.6%). In contrast, three of the four big banks managed modest advances.

【紐西蘭不對鋁型材進口實施保障措施】紐西蘭商務部長卡梅倫・布魯爾在電子郵件宣告中表示，紐西蘭決定不對鋁型材進口徵收保障措施。本土產業稱進口產品造成損害後，紐西蘭商業、創新與就業部（MBIE）官員完成調查，結論是綜合公共利益考量，不應實施保障措施。布魯爾採納了該建議。他稱，商業、創新與就業部發現該行業確實面臨困境，進口加劇了相關壓力。“但調查同時發現，進口只是行業困境的眾多誘因之一，建築活動低迷、生產成本上升等因素同樣影響顯著。”商業、創新與就業部報告指出，實施保障措施或將推高下游製造企業、終端使用者與消費者的成本，也不利於紐西蘭的國際關係與貿易目標。布魯爾表示，該行業仍可另行申請反傾銷或反補貼稅調查，核查進口產品是否存在定價不公或獲得補貼的情況。他稱，如果行業打算走上述途徑，商業、創新與就業部願意與其開展建設性協作。

Japan Producer Prices Rise the Least in 4 Months — Japan’s producer prices rose 6.8% year-on-year in September 2026, slowing from August’s pace and market expectations of 7.6% and marking the weakest growth since May. The moderation suggests easing upstream cost pressures, although producer inflation remained elevated. Prices moderated for transport equipment (2.3% vs 2.5% in August), drinks and foods (4.2% vs 4.3%), petroleum & coal (11.5% vs 14.7%), production machinery (4.3% vs 4.5%), and textiles (1.4% vs 2.2%). Meanwhile, costs accelerated for chemicals (13.3% vs 13.0%), iron & steel (3.2% vs 2.7%), electrical machinery (5.1% vs 4.9%), metal products (5.1% vs 3.8%), electronic components (4.9% vs 4.4%), and information and communications (15.3% vs 14.8%).On a monthly basis, producer prices fell 0.3%, matching the revised decline in August and marking a second consecutive monthly contraction. The result also defied market expectations for a 0.5% increase.

### 2026-10-13 07:56:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Loan Growth #Importance 1

Japan Bank Lending Growth Below Forecasts — Japan’s bank lending grew by 5.3% year-on-year in September 2026, matching the downwardly revised figure for August and falling short of market expectations for a 5.5% rise. Outstanding loans at major, regional, and shinkin banks averaged JPY 681.97 trillion. Lending at major and regional banks rose 5.7%, easing from 5.8% in August, with major-bank lending growth slowing to 7.7% from 7.8%, while regional-bank lending growth stood at 4.1%. Within regional banks, lending growth held steady at 4.5% for regional banks I, while growth at regional banks II eased to 1.9% from 2.0%. Meanwhile, lending by shinkin banks held steady at 2.0%, remaining at its strongest growth rate since early 2023. Foreign-bank lending growth slowed to 14.5% from 25.6% in August.

【央地國企整合升溫 專家稱戰略性新興產業或成佈局重點】“十五五”開局之年，“央地和鳴”成為國有企業資產整合的新風向。2026年，已有六組央地國企上市公司的整合案例出現，涵蓋央企入主地方國資旗下上市公司、地方國資接棒央企控股上市公司控股權、地方國企反向入股央企，以及不涉及控制權變更的“資產換股權”戰略聯姻，交易結構的多樣性與創新性均超出以往。年內的央地資產整合集中出現在戰略資源、高階製造、公共安全、醫藥健康等關鍵領域，標的資產大多處於產業鏈上游或核心環節，指向服務國家戰略、保障產業鏈安全、佈局戰略性新興產業。清華大學中國現代國有企業研究院研究主任周麗莎對記者表示，新一輪戰略性重組不再單純追求企業體量，而是將能否補強國家戰略短板、引領產業升級作為核心，明確同類業務橫向整合、產業鏈上下游縱向整合，提升國有資本整體配置效率。她進一步判斷，戰略性新興產業將成為下一步佈局的核心重點，聚焦新一代資訊科技、新能源、新材料、低空經濟等前沿領域展開。（上證報）

【四家期交所出手 劍指程式化交易】10月12日，上海期貨交易所（含上期能源）、鄭州商品交易所、大連商品交易所、廣州期貨交易所同時釋出公告，就修訂交易所違規處理辦法公開徵求意見，反饋截止日期為10月19日。此次修訂的核心，是落實《期貨和衍生品法》及《期貨市場程式化交易管理規定（試行）》，把程式化交易監管要求寫入交易所違規處理規則。業內人士指出，4家期交所同步徵求意見，釋放出一個明確訊號：程式化交易是期貨市場一線監管的重點場景。規則完善後，異常交易、高頻套利、跨所報撤等行為將面臨更統一的認定標準和更硬的自律懲戒。期貨程式化交易監管已完成“定義—報告—接入—監測”階段，正在進入“違規—認定—處分”可執行階段。（證券時報）

【供需“緊平衡”引發電子布10月再漲價 覆銅板成本向PCB順暢傳導】多家機構資料顯示，10月份，電子布價格延續上漲趨勢，環比漲幅超過10%。國金證券研報顯示，10月以來，電子布價格再次落地1.5元/米漲幅（7628型號），電子紗G75上漲3000元/噸，薄布（2116、1080型號）上漲2.5元/米。多家電子布行業頭部上市公司接受記者採訪時表示，電子布行業整體處於庫存緊平衡狀態，薄布漲價幅度更大。今年以來，中國巨石、建滔集團投建的電子紗、電子布產能加速落地，一定程度上緩解了市場供給壓力。此外，行業頭部公司紛紛加碼電子布產能，由於建設週期普遍在18個月左右，預計2027年年底之前新增產能將陸續落地。下游AI需求旺盛的情況下，電子布有望延續高景氣週期。此外，多家覆銅板上市公司告訴記者，對於覆銅板企業來說，上游電子布漲價會導致成本上升，但由於下游印刷線路板（PCB）需求旺盛，原材料漲價可以有效傳導到下游去。至於PCB漲價是否可以傳導到終端，有覆銅板上市公司告訴記者，對於很多終端產品來說，PCB的成本佔比很小，除了PCB以外，電容、電阻以及線材等電子元器件價格也都在上漲。算力建設對這類產品的需求目前整體比較旺盛，行業漲價邏輯沒有發生變化。（中國證券報）

UK Retail Sales Growth Picks Up in September — UK retail sales rose by 1.0% year-on-year on a like-for-like basis in September 2026, exceeding forecasts for a 0.7% gain and accelerating from 0.5% in the previous month. Total retail sales growth also picked up to 1.3% from 0.7%, but remained below the 12-month average of 1.5%. Food sales increased by 2.5%, while non-food sales edged up by 0.3%. Health and beauty products recorded their strongest growth of the year, while clothing and footwear sales weakened due to unseasonably warm weather. Separately, Barclays' measure of consumer spending rose 2.0% year-on-year, slowing from 2.1% in August. Essential spending climbed 2.5%, driven by a 17.3% jump in fuel spending as diesel prices reached record highs, while non-essential spending increased 1.8%. Household financial confidence rose to a seven-month high of 67%, although 68% remained concerned about the upcoming budget's impact on their finances and 30% were cutting back on non-essential spending.

### 2026-10-13 07:16:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Jordan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Jordan Inflation Rate Hits 3-Month High — Jordan's annual inflation rate accelerated for the first time in four months to 2.73% in September 2026, up slightly from a four-month low of 2.66% in August. It was the highest inflation rate since June, with transportation prices making the largest contribution to inflation, at 1.13 percentage points. Jordan will raise fuel prices in October after freezing them in September despite soaring global crude prices. However, price declines in several food categories helped offset inflationary pressures during September. For the first nine months of the year, the inflation rate averaged 2.26%, with rent being the largest contributor to inflation, adding 0.72 percentage points to the overall rate. On a monthly basis, the CPI rose 0.16% in September, marking the first monthly increase in three months, after falling 0.25% in August.

【UK retail growth weak in September as fuel spending surges】UK consumers held back overall spending in September, with most incremental outlays going to fuel while warm weather supported pubs and depressed clothing sales, the British Retail Consortium and Barclays said. The BRC said total retail sales rose 1.3% YoY in September, up from 0.7% in August but below the 12-month average of 1.5%. Barclays’ card-spend series showed annual growth of 2.0% in September, down from 2.1% in August. Barclays’ household financial confidence index climbed to a seven-month high of 67, yet 68% of consumers said they were concerned about Chancellor John Healey’s Oct. 28 budget and 30% reported cutting discretionary spending.

【人民日報：若不切斷流量利益鏈，“飯圈”亂象很容易換個陣地形式捲土重來】“飯圈”亂象之所以成為頑疾，主因在於其背後成熟的流量套路與利益閉環。資本與營銷號靠製造對立、煽動焦慮牟利，粉頭牽頭組織、裹挾低齡群體衝鋒，平臺演算法助推衝突傳播，形成“幕後賺流量、粉絲擔風險”的畸形模式。如果不切斷流量利益鏈，“飯圈”亂象很容易換個陣地、換種形式捲土重來。杜絕體育“飯圈”化，需全社會協同發力，形成治理閉環。法律要持續亮明底線，對圍堵、網暴、組織滋事等行為依法從嚴懲處，深挖幕後煽動者與利益鏈，以剛性約束築牢紅線。網路平臺要切實扛起主體責任，最佳化演算法導向，嚴控對立內容傳播，阻斷戾氣蔓延。家庭與學校要加強青少年法治與媒介素養教育，幫助孩子認清“飯圈”套路，不被圈層裹挾。社會輿論要凝聚共識，倡導文明觀賽，讓體育正能量成為主流。

NZX 50 Edges Lower in Morning Trade, — The NZX 50 dropped 26 points, or 0.2%, to 13,801 in Tuesday morning trade, erasing gains from the previous session, mainly weighed down by industrials, communication services, utilities, technology, and financials. The broader index tracked a decline in the Nasdaq on Wall Street overnight, led by losses in the tech sector. Rising oil prices pressured sentiment amid heightened concerns over inflation and interest rate hikes. Caution mounted ahead of the release of New Zealand's food inflation and business PMI data due later this week. Traders also awaited US inflation data for clues about the Fed's interest rate decision at its upcoming meeting, as well as China's September CPI, PPI, and trade data amid weak domestic demand and rising oil prices. Among the early losers were Freightways Group (-2.2%), AFT Pharmaceuticals (-2.0%), Auckland International Airport (-1.4%), Skellerup Holdings (-1.3%), Mainfreight (-1.0%), Chorus (-1.0%), and Infratil (-0.9%).

### 2026-10-13 04:40:16  #公司

【葉門胡塞武裝再襲沙特首都機場並打擊一處空軍基地】葉門胡塞武裝12日說，該組織當天向沙烏地阿拉伯首都利雅得的哈立德國王國際機場發射一枚彈道導彈，並使用無人機襲擊沙特西南部一處空軍基地。胡塞武裝發言人葉海亞·薩雷亞當天在社交媒體發表宣告說，該組織12日晚向利雅得哈立德國王國際機場發射的導彈命中目標；該組織還使用無人機襲擊了位於沙特西南部海米斯穆謝特的哈立德國王空軍基地，無人機也命中目標。薩雷亞再次警告國際航空公司、航空從業人員及旅客不要使用沙特境內的機場和領空，稱除麥加和麥地那的機場及空域外，沙特機場均“不安全”。近日，胡塞武裝連續襲擊沙特境內的機場和石油設施。沙特主導的多國聯軍則表示，正開展大規模軍事行動，打擊胡塞武裝人員及其軍事能力，著力瓦解其彈道導彈網路，並全天候為葉門政府軍提供空中支援。（新華社）

US Stocks End Mixed, With Tech Shares Under Pressure — The S&P 500 closed little changed and the Nasdaq fell 0.4%, while the Dow gained 196 points on Monday. Tech led the declines, while energy, communication services and consumer staples outperformed. Investors are preparing for the earnings season while awaiting key US inflation data due later this week. Meanwhile, oil prices rose following deadly strikes on Riyadh’s airport over the weekend. Prices also drew support from renewed concerns over energy infrastructure after Ukrainian President Zelenskyy said Russia had continued targeting energy facilities, despite US President Trump’s highlighting fresh Russian diesel exports and a Russia-Ukraine ceasefire on energy targets. Micron tumbled 3.9% as investors assessed growing competition from China’s CXMT and Nvidia fell 1.3%. In contrast, Meta gained 3.4% following the release of a long-awaited AI model, while Salesforce advanced 2.1% after Bank of America upgraded the stock. Chevron also rose 1.4% amid higher oil prices.

【美國9月二手房簽約量創近十二年最低 房貸利率飆升重挫購房需求】隨著房貸利率重返7%以上，美國房地產市場進一步降溫。上月賣家紛紛降價，為數不多的買家則掌握了更多議價權。房地產經紀公司Redfin經季節性調整的資料顯示，9月二手房簽約量環比大跌4%至2014年以來最低水平，降幅創逾三年來最大（2020年新冠疫情初期的幾個月除外）。與此同時，待售房屋供應量升至2020年3月以來最高水平。Redfin首席經濟學家Daryl Fairweather表示：“房地產行業的人氣明顯轉變。利率像這樣上升時，能夠負擔得起買房的人會減少，這意味著銷量必然下降。”房地美資料顯示，30年期房貸平均利率從8月底的6.66%躍升至10月8日的7.4%，創近三年來最高水平，並較上年同期高出逾1個百分點。
